# Strata command reference

Generated by `strata-devtool docs`; edit the Rust registry and parameter types, then regenerate. Examples use host fixture aliases, never arbitrary model-supplied paths.

## `file.new` — 新建文档

Create one artboard and a pixel background. Supply positive width/height or preset web_1080p, a4_300dpi, photo_4x6_300dpi, square_2048 (explicit dimensions must match). bit_depth is 8/16; resolution_dpi is integer 1..2400 (preset default, otherwise 72); working_space is srgb/display_p3; background is optional RGBA at document depth, otherwise sparse transparent. Preset metadata is kept only for exact geometry/density. Replacing dirty content requires discard_changes.

Kind: `Lifecycle` · Undoable: `false` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "NewDocument",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "background": {
      "type": [
        "array",
        "null"
      ],
      "default": null,
      "items": {
        "type": "integer",
        "format": "uint16",
        "maximum": 65535,
        "minimum": 0
      },
      "maxItems": 4,
      "minItems": 4
    },
    "bit_depth": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint8",
      "maximum": 255,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "discard_changes": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "height": {
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "uint32",
      "default": null,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "preset": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "default": null
    },
    "resolution_dpi": {
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "uint32",
      "default": null,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "width": {
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "uint32",
      "default": null,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "working_space": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "default": null
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "bit_depth"
  ]
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "NewResult",

      "type": "string",

  "required": [

  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "bit_depth": 16,
  "height": 16,
  "width": 16
}
```

```json
{
  "background": [
    65535,
    65535,
    65535,
    65535
  ],
  "bit_depth": 16,
  "preset": "a4_300dpi",
  "working_space": "display_p3"
}
```

```json
{
  "background": [
    10,
    20,
    30,
    128
  ],
  "bit_depth": 8,
  "height": 96,
  "resolution_dpi": 150,
  "width": 128
}
```

## `document.inspect` — 文档信息

Read metadata, revision and saved state for the bound document.

Kind: `Query` · Undoable: `false` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.read`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Empty",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## `file.recent.list` — 最近文件

Read host-provided recent metadata (maximum 10). User/Test sees host history; AI sees only entries with a still-valid read resource for that exact turn/source in this editor session, with paths omitted. Listing never grants resources. Availability is advisory. Allowed with no document; not batchable.

Kind: `Query` · Undoable: `false` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `history.read, file.metadata.read`

Permissions: `host_recent_visibility`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Empty",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "RecentListResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "files": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/$defs/RecentEntry"
      }
    },
    "scope": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "scope",
    "files"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "RecentEntry": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "available": {
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "id": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "name": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "path": {
          "type": [
            "string",
            "null"
          ]
        },
        "read_resource": {
          "type": [
            "string",
            "null"
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "id",
        "name",
        "available"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Examples

```json
{}
```

## `layer.list` — 图层列表

Read layers in bottom-to-top order. Stable IDs are preferred over names.

Kind: `Query` · Undoable: `false` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.read`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Empty",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

```json
              "format": "double"

              "format": "double"

                "minimum": 0
```

## `document.histogram` — 亮度直方图

Read a bounded 256-bin linear luminance histogram of the visible composite, sampled at up to 256 by 256 pixel centers. It is a display statistic, not document samples.

Kind: `Query` · Undoable: `false` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.read`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Empty",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "HistogramResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "luminance": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "integer",
        "format": "uint64",
        "minimum": 0
      }
    },
    "method": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "nontransparent_pixels": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "sampled_pixels": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint",
      "minimum": 0
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "luminance",
    "sampled_pixels",
    "nontransparent_pixels",
    "method"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{}
```

## `layer.create` — 新建图层

Create an artboard-sized pixel layer. Optional fill is RGBA integers at document depth; otherwise sparse transparent.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "CreateLayer",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "fill": {
      "type": [
        "array",
        "null"
      ],
      "default": null,
      "items": {
        "type": "integer",
        "format": "uint16",
        "maximum": 65535,
        "minimum": 0
      },
      "maxItems": 4,
      "minItems": 4
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "name"
  ]
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "LayerResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layer_id"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "name": "New layer"
}
```

## `layer.delete` — 删除图层

Delete a pixel layer. Undo restores its stable ID and samples.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Target",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
    }
  },
```

## `layer.set_blend_mode` — 混合模式

Choose one of 27 registered blend modes. Blending operates on linear Display P3; source document samples remain unchanged. Dissolve uses deterministic canvas-coordinate and layer-ID noise, so repeats never flicker.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Blend",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "blend_mode": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/BlendMode"
    },
    "layer": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layer",
    "blend_mode"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "BlendMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "normal",
        "multiply",
        "screen",
        "overlay",
        "dissolve",
        "darken",
        "color_burn",
        "linear_burn",
        "darker_color",
        "lighten",
        "color_dodge",
        "linear_dodge",
        "lighter_color",
        "soft_light",
        "hard_light",
        "vivid_light",
        "linear_light",
        "pin_light",
        "hard_mix",
        "difference",
        "exclusion",
        "subtract",
        "divide",
        "hue",
        "saturation",
        "color",
        "luminosity"
      ]
    },
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "LayerResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layer_id"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "blend_mode": "multiply",
  "layer": {
    "name": "Background"
  }
}
```

## `layer.set_locks` — 图层锁定

Set transparency/pixels/position/all locks. Full lock blocks edits except visibility and this unlock operation. Pixel operations and source color conversion respect locks at the command boundary.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "SetLocks",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
    },
    "locks": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerLocks"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layer",
    "locks"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "LayerLocks": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "all": {
          "type": "boolean",
          "default": false
        },
        "pixels": {
          "type": "boolean",
          "default": false
        },
        "position": {
          "type": "boolean",
          "default": false
        },
        "transparency": {
          "type": "boolean",
          "default": false
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "LayerResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layer_id"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "layer": {
    "name": "Background"
  },
  "locks": {
    "position": true
  }
}
```

## `select.marquee` — 矩形选区

Replace, add, subtract or intersect a bounded 16-bit coverage selection. Coordinates are canvas pixels and out-of-artboard area is clipped.

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Marquee",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint32",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "mode": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/SelectionMode",
      "default": "replace"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint32",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "x": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    },
    "y": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "x",
    "y",
    "width",
    "height"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "SelectionMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "replace",
        "add",
        "subtract",
        "intersect"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "SelectionResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "selection": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/MaskLayout"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "$defs": {
    "CoverageRect": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "height": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint32",
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "width": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint32",
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "x": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64"
        },
        "y": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "x",
        "y",
        "width",
        "height"
      ]
    },
    "MaskLayout": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "bounds": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/CoverageRect"
        },
        "default_coverage": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint16",
          "maximum": 65535,
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "inverted": {
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "bounds",
        "default_coverage",
        "inverted"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "height": 100,
  "mode": "replace",
  "width": 100,
  "x": 0,
  "y": 0
}
```

## `select.subject` — 选择主体

Run the host's checksum-verified local ONNX subject model. Optional region must be inside the artboard; confidence/multiple-subject ambiguity requires asking the user before accept_ambiguous=true. This does not identify people. Missing assets require explicit host download approval; inference is cancellable and cannot be batched.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document, verified_local_subject_model`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "SubjectParams",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "accept_ambiguous": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "mode": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/SelectionMode",
      "default": "replace"
    },
    "region": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/CoverageRect"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ],
      "default": null
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "$defs": {
    "CoverageRect": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "height": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint32",
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "width": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint32",
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "x": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64"
        },
        "y": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "x",
        "y",
        "width",
        "height"
      ]
    },
    "SelectionMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "replace",
        "add",
        "subtract",
        "intersect"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "SubjectResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "diagnostics": true,
    "selection": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/MaskLayout"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "diagnostics"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "CoverageRect": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "height": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint32",
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "width": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint32",
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "x": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64"
        },
        "y": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "x",
        "y",
        "width",
        "height"
      ]
    },
    "MaskLayout": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "bounds": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/CoverageRect"
        },
        "default_coverage": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint16",
          "maximum": 65535,
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "inverted": {
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,

}
```

## `layer.mask.add` — 添加图层蒙版

Add a sparse 16-bit white, black or current-selection mask. The source pixels remain unchanged.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "AddMask",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
    },
    "source": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/MaskPreset",
      "default": "white"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layer"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    },
    "MaskPreset": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "white",
        "black",
        "from_selection"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "LayerResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layer_id"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "layer": {
    "name": "Background"
  },
  "source": "white"
}
```

## `layer.add_adjustment` — 新建曝光调整层

Create a non-destructive exposure adjustment layer with stops in -20..20 and a white, black or selection mask.

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",

      "$ref": "#/$defs/MaskPreset",
      "default": "white"
    },
    "name": {

    },

      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 0.0
    },
    "type": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/AdjustmentType"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "name",
    "type"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "AdjustmentType": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "exposure"
      ]
    },
    "MaskPreset": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "white",
        "black",
        "from_selection"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

```json
  "properties": {

      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layer_id"
  ]
}
```

```json
  "name": "人物提亮",
  "stops": 0.5,
  "type": "exposure"
}
```

## `document.batch` — 批量修改

Preflight all steps. Only same-document query/mutate steps are allowed; results can be referenced with {$ref:{step:0,field:layer_id}}.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

```json
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Batch",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "steps": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/$defs/BatchStep"
      }
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "steps"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "BatchStep": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "command": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "params": true
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "command",
        "params"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "BatchResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "results": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": true
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "results"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
  "steps": [
    {
      "command": "layer.create",
      "params": {
        "name": "Batch layer"
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

## `file.export` — 导出图片

Export PNG/TIFF at document depth or JPEG/WebP at 8-bit. WebP defaults to lossless; webp_lossless=false enables lossy RGB with lossless alpha, quality 1..100 (default 80). JPEG quality is 1..100 (default 90) and requires an 8-bit RGB background in output space. quality is rejected for lossless WebP; webp_lossless is rejected for non-WebP. Optional width/height must both be positive, bounded to 512 MiB output samples; WebP dimensions <=16383. Resize uses linear-light premultiplied area reduction / bilinear enlargement. target_space is optional srgb/display_p3, otherwise keep working ICC. All outputs include document DPI. 16-bit to JPEG/WebP requires allow_quantization=true. Source samples/history/dirty state do not change.

Kind: `External` · Undoable: `false` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.read, file.write`

Permissions: `bound_document, write_resource`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "ExportFile",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "allow_quantization": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "background": {
      "type": [
        "array",
        "null"
      ],
      "default": null,
      "items": {
        "type": "integer",
        "format": "uint8",
        "maximum": 255,
        "minimum": 0
      },
      "maxItems": 3,
      "minItems": 3
    },
    "format": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "height": {
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "uint32",
      "default": null,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "quality": {
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "uint8",
      "default": null,
      "maximum": 255,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "resource": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "target_space": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "default": null
    },
    "webp_lossless": {
      "type": [
        "boolean",
        "null"
      ],
      "default": null
    },
    "width": {
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "uint32",
      "default": null,
      "minimum": 0
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "resource",
    "format"
  ]
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "ExternalResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "command": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "status": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "status",
    "command"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "format": "png",
  "resource": "fixture-export"
}
```

```json
{
  "allow_quantization": true,
  "format": "webp",
  "quality": 80,
  "resource": "fixture-export",
  "webp_lossless": false
}
```

```json
{
  "format": "png",
  "height": 4,
  "resource": "fixture-export",
  "width": 8
}
```

```json
{
  "format": "tiff",
  "resource": "fixture-export",
  "target_space": "display_p3"
}
```

```json
{
  "allow_quantization": true,
  "background": [
    255,
    255,
    255
  ],
  "format": "jpeg",
  "quality": 90,
  "resource": "fixture-export",
  "target_space": "srgb"
}
```

```json
{
  "allow_quantization": true,
  "format": "webp",
  "resource": "fixture-export"
}
```

## `layer.group` — 编组

Group a contiguous range of unlocked siblings into a pass-through group, preserving sibling z order. Child canvas coordinates remain unchanged.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "GroupLayers",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layers": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
      }
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layers",
    "name"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "LayerResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layer_id"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json

  "layers": [
    {
      "name": "Background"
    }
  ],
  "name": "Group"
```
```json
}
```

## `layer.reparent` — 移动到组

Move a layer or subtree to an unlocked group or null root, using optional zero-based sibling index. Reject cycles and excessive depth; absolute canvas positions are preserved.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Reparent",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "index": {
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "uint",
      "default": null,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "layer": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
    },
    "parent": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layer"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
```

## `layer.fill.solid` — 新建纯色填充

Create an editable artboard-sized fill using document-depth RGBA. Colors are interpreted in the working ICC. No source pixel blocks are allocated.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Permissions: `bound_document`

```json
      "items": {
        "type": "integer",
        "format": "uint16",
        "maximum": 65535,
        "minimum": 0
      },
      "maxItems": 4,
      "minItems": 4
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [

    "color"
  ]
}
```

## `layer.fill.gradient` — 新建渐变填充

Create a linear (angle degrees) or radial fill. start/end are RGBA at document depth; scale 0.01..100. Interpolate in linear light with premultiplied alpha.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

```json
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 0.0
    },
    "end": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "integer",
        "format": "uint16",
        "maximum": 65535,
        "minimum": 0
      },
      "maxItems": 4,
      "minItems": 4
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "radial": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": false
    },
    "scale": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 1.0
    },
    "start": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "integer",
        "format": "uint16",
        "maximum": 65535,
        "minimum": 0
      },
      "maxItems": 4,
      "minItems": 4
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "name",
    "start",
    "end"
  ]
}
```

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "LayerResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layer_id"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json

  "angle": 0,
  "end": [

    65535,
    65535,
    65535
  ],

    0,
    65535
  ]
}
```

## `layer.align` — 对齐图层

Align unlocked independent layers/subtrees by exact content bounds to the selection or artboard. Modes: left/horizontal_center/right/top/vertical_center/bottom. No selected ancestor/descendant overlap.

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

```json
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "AlignLayers",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layers": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
      }
    },
    "mode": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/AlignMode"
    },
    "reference": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/AlignReference",
      "default": "selection"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layers",
    "mode"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "AlignMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "left",
        "horizontal_center",
        "right",
        "top",
        "vertical_center",
        "bottom"
      ]
    },
    "AlignReference": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "selection",
        "artboard"
      ]
    },
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "MultiLayerResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer_ids": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "format": "uuid"
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layer_ids"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "layers": [
    {
      "name": "Background"
    }
  ],
  "mode": "horizontal_center",
  "reference": "artboard"
}
```

## `layer.distribute` — 分布图层

Distribute at least three unlocked layers by horizontal/vertical centers or equal spacing, preserving the endpoint positions. Supports groups and generated fills.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "DistributeLayers",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layers": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
      }
    },
    "mode": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/Distribution"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layers",
    "mode"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "Distribution": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "horizontal_centers",
        "vertical_centers",
        "horizontal_spacing",
        "vertical_spacing"
      ]
    },
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "MultiLayerResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer_ids": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "format": "uuid"
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layer_ids"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "layers": [
    {
      "name": "Background"
    },
    {
      "name": "Second"
    },
    {
      "name": "Third"
    }
  ],
  "mode": "horizontal_centers"
}
```

## `layer.set_styles` — 图层样式

Replace the optional drop_shadow, stroke, outer_glow, color_overlay and gradient_overlay settings. All RGBA colors use document depth/ICC. Shadow blur is Gaussian sigma 0..32 and offsets ±256; stroke/glow sizes 0..64. Content fill opacity does not fade effects; overall layer opacity does. Masks are applied before styles; effects are normal source-over except overlay colors' blend modes.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "SetStyles",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
    },
    "styles": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerStyles"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layer",
    "styles"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "BlendMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "normal",
        "multiply",
        "screen",
        "overlay",
        "dissolve",
        "darken",
        "color_burn",
        "linear_burn",
        "darker_color",
        "lighten",
        "color_dodge",
        "linear_dodge",
        "lighter_color",
        "soft_light",
        "hard_light",
        "vivid_light",
        "linear_light",
        "pin_light",
        "hard_mix",
        "difference",
        "exclusion",
        "subtract",
        "divide",
        "hue",
        "saturation",
        "color",
        "luminosity"
      ]
    },
    "Glow": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "color": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "integer",
            "format": "uint16",
            "maximum": 65535,
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "maxItems": 4,
          "minItems": 4
        },
        "size": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "color",
        "size"
      ]
    },
    "GradientOverlay": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "angle": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double"
        },
        "blend_mode": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/BlendMode",
          "default": "normal"
        },
        "end": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "integer",
            "format": "uint16",
            "maximum": 65535,
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "maxItems": 4,
          "minItems": 4
        },
        "opacity": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double",
          "default": 1.0
        },
        "radial": {
          "type": "boolean",
          "default": false
        },
        "scale": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double",
          "default": 1.0
        },
        "start": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "integer",
            "format": "uint16",
            "maximum": 65535,
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "maxItems": 4,
          "minItems": 4
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "start",
        "end",
        "angle"
      ]
    },
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    },
    "LayerStyles": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "color_overlay": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/$defs/Overlay"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ],
          "default": null
        },
        "drop_shadow": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/$defs/Shadow"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ],
          "default": null
        },
        "gradient_overlay": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/$defs/GradientOverlay"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ],
          "default": null
        },
        "outer_glow": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/$defs/Glow"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ],
          "default": null
        },
        "stroke": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/$defs/Stroke"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ],
          "default": null
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "Overlay": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "blend_mode": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/BlendMode",
          "default": "normal"
        },
        "color": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "integer",
            "format": "uint16",
            "maximum": 65535,
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "maxItems": 4,
          "minItems": 4
        },
        "opacity": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double",
          "default": 1.0
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "color"
      ]
    },
    "Shadow": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "blur": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double"
        },
        "color": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "integer",
            "format": "uint16",
            "maximum": 65535,
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "maxItems": 4,
          "minItems": 4
        },
        "offset_x": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int32"
        },
        "offset_y": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int32"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "color",
        "offset_x",
        "offset_y",
        "blur"
      ]
    },
    "Stroke": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "color": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "integer",
            "format": "uint16",
            "maximum": 65535,
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "maxItems": 4,
          "minItems": 4
        },
        "position": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/StrokePosition",
          "default": "outside"
        },
        "size": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "color",
        "size"
      ]
    },
    "StrokePosition": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "outside",
        "inside",
        "center"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "LayerResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "layer_id"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "layer": {
    "name": "Background"
  },
  "styles": {
    "stroke": {
      "color": [
        65535,
        65535,
        65535,
        65535
      ],
      "position": "outside",
      "size": 2
    }
  }
}
```

## `edit.copy` — 拷贝

Prepare a bounded selection/artboard copy of the active or specified layer. Returns an opaque source-bound clipboard resource; the trusted host must write its payload to the system clipboard and acknowledge completion. No document history.

Kind: `External` · Undoable: `false` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.read, clipboard.write`

Permissions: `bound_document, clipboard.write_confirmation`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "PixelTarget",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ],
      "default": null
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "$defs": {
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "ClipboardCopyResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "clipboard_resource": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "status": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "undoable": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "clipboard_resource",
    "status",
    "undoable"
  ]
}
```

### Examples

```json
{}
```

Clear the source alpha only after the host confirms a copy resource was written to the clipboard. The receipt must match this exact source, document state and original layer. Clipboard contents are not reversed by document undo.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

```json
{

  "properties": {

        }
```
```json
      ],

    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "$defs": {
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
```

## `layer.set_pass_through` — 组穿透模式

Choose normal pass-through or isolated group compositing. Pass-through evaluates child blend/adjustment effects against the parent backdrop, then interpolates with the group mask/opacity. Non-normal group blend or group styles use isolated compositing.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "PassThrough",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
    },
    "pass_through": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layer",
    "pass_through"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",

  "properties": {

      "type": "string",

    }
  },
  "required": [

}
```

```json
    "name": "Group"
```

## `select.ellipse` — 椭圆选框

Select an antialiased ellipse in a canvas pixel rectangle, using 16-bit 4x4 edge coverage. Mode replace/add/subtract/intersect; artboard clips the result. Cancellable, not batchable.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Marquee",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "height": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint32",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "mode": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/SelectionMode",
      "default": "replace"
    },
    "width": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint32",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "x": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    },
    "y": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "x",
    "y",
    "width",
    "height"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "SelectionMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "replace",
        "add",
        "subtract",
        "intersect"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "SelectionResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "selection": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/MaskLayout"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "$defs": {
    "CoverageRect": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "height": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint32",
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "width": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint32",
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "x": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64"
        },
        "y": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "x",
        "y",
        "width",
        "height"
      ]
    },
    "MaskLayout": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "bounds": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/CoverageRect"
        },
        "default_coverage": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint16",
          "maximum": 65535,
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "inverted": {
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "bounds",
        "default_coverage",
        "inverted"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "height": 8,
  "mode": "replace",
  "width": 8,
  "x": 2,
  "y": 2
}
```

## `select.polygon` — 多边形套索

Close a finite 3..4096-point polygon using the even-odd fill rule and 4x4 coverage, in canvas coordinates. Self intersections are supported. Cancellable and bounded by artboard.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "PolygonSelection",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "mode": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/SelectionMode",
      "default": "replace"
    },
    "points": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "array",
        "items": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double"
        },
        "maxItems": 2,
        "minItems": 2
      }
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "points"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "SelectionMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "replace",
        "add",
        "subtract",
        "intersect"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "SelectionResult",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "selection": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/MaskLayout"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "$defs": {
    "CoverageRect": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "height": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint32",
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "width": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint32",
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "x": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64"
        },
        "y": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "x",
        "y",
        "width",
        "height"
      ]
    },
    "MaskLayout": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "bounds": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/CoverageRect"
        },
        "default_coverage": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "uint16",
          "maximum": 65535,
          "minimum": 0
        },
        "inverted": {
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "bounds",
        "default_coverage",
        "inverted"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Examples

```json
{
  "points": [
    [
      1,
      1
    ],
    [
      12,
      1
    ],
    [
      8,
      12
    ]
  ]
}
```

## `select.feather` — 羽化选区

```json
}
```

## `select.smooth` — 平滑选区

## `select.border` — 选区边界

## `select.wand` — 魔棒

Select colors within an 8-bit-scaled maximum RGBA-channel tolerance (0..255). Sample visible merged working-space color or raw current pixel layer; transparent RGB is ignored. Contiguous uses four-connected flood fill. Seed is in artboard canvas pixels; all four selection modes, cancellation and shared scratch budget apply.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "contiguous": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "mode": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/SelectionMode",
      "default": "replace"
    },
    "sample": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/SelectionSample",
      "default": "merged"
    },
    "tolerance": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint8",
      "default": 32,
      "maximum": 255,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "x": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    },
    "y": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "x",
    "y"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "SelectionMode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "replace",
        "add",
        "subtract",
        "intersect"
      ]
    },
    "SelectionSample": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "merged",
        "current_layer"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

## `select.quick` — 快速选择

Seeded Gaussian color graph cut on a pixel-center preview of at most 256 pixels on the longest edge. Foreground/background are canvas-coordinate brush points, radius 0.5..256 pixels, smoothness 0..100. Without background strokes, unmarked preview borders supply background. Conflicting preview seeds are rejected. Bilinear upsampling produces u16 coverage. Sample merged or raw current pixel layer; source pixels stay unchanged. Cancellable and not batchable; this is traditional segmentation, not semantic ML.

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

```json
      "format": "double",

    },
```

## `layer.add_tonal_adjustment` — 新建调色调整层

Create a typed adjustment with the current selection as mask (white if none). Floating encoded document working RGB, source samples and alpha preserved. brightness_contrast: -100..100, additive brightness /100 then contrast about 0.5 (negative gain 1+c; positive 1/(1-c); +100 threshold). levels: master then RGB, normalized black/white and gamma 0.1..10. curves: master then RGB PCHIP, 2..64 points, x strictly increasing from 0 to 1, y 0..1. vibrance: vibrance/saturation -100..100, prioritize low saturation and reduce enhancement near skin hue. color_balance: shadows/midtones/highlights RGB axes -100..100, Rec.709 encoded-luma range weights; preserve_luminance gamut-clips while retaining luma. black_white: six hue sensitivities R/Y/G/C/B/M -200..300, optional normalized tint and tint_amount 0..100. photo_filter: normalized RGB transmittance color, density 0..100 and optional luma preservation. channel_mixer: 3x4 normalized coefficients -2..2 with constants, optional monochrome gray row. invert: 1-RGB. posterize: 2..65536 levels/channel. threshold: encoded Rec.709-weighted level 0..1, equality maps white. gradient_map: 2..64 strictly ordered normalized stops spanning 0..1, encoded RGB interpolation and optional reverse. selective_color: nine CMYK percentage rows R/Y/G/C/B/M/white/neutral/black, -100..100; relative scales by channel/headroom and absolute uses direct offsets. desaturate: HSL lightness (min+max)/2. Neutral settings of brightness/levels/curves/vibrance/balance/mixer/selective color are exact identity in normal mode. One undoable edit; manifest v4. white_balance: temperature/tint -100..100; positive temperature warms using reciprocal-Kelvin shift relative to D65 (6504K) and Bradford adaptation in linear Display P3, positive tint reduces the M cone response toward magenta; optional physical Y preservation. White balance and local tone are exceptions to encoded working RGB operations. shadows_highlights: shadow/highlight amounts 0..100, widths 0.01..1, radius 0..256 (sigma=radius/3). Physical linear P3 Y and alpha-normalized three-box smoothing estimate local tone on the actual lower input stack. The finite kernel support defines the cross-tile halo; artboard edges clamp to the nearest valid sample. Linear gain is up to two stops in shadows/highlights, then mask/opacity/blend apply. Both strengths zero is exact identity; cancellation and shared temporary budgets apply. 中文检索：亮度、对比度、色阶、曲线、自然饱和度、色温、色调、色彩平衡、黑白、照片滤镜、通道混合器、反相、色调分离、阈值、渐变映射、可选颜色、去色、阴影、高光。

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

## `adjust.tonal.set` — 设置调色调整层

Replace complete tonal settings of an existing adjustment layer without changing its type. Same strict normalized parameters as layer.add_tonal_adjustment. Omitted fields reset to that type's defaults; specify the complete intended settings. Masks, alpha and original source samples are preserved; lock and ancestor checks apply. 中文检索：亮度、对比度、色阶、曲线、自然饱和度、色温、色调、色彩平衡、黑白、照片滤镜、通道混合器、反相、色调分离、阈值、渐变映射、可选颜色、去色、阴影、高光。

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `true`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

## `paint.stroke` — 画笔笔触

Paint one whole stroke on an explicit pixel layer or existing u16 mask. Points are finite canvas x/y and pressure 0..1 (default 1), at most 8192. Size 0.5..4096 canvas pixels; hardness/opacity/flow/value 0..1; spacing 0.01..2 diameters. Round tip, smooth soft falloff and antialiased boundary. Arc-length dab placement is independent of input event density; pressure_size scales diameter to at least 0.25px and pressure_opacity caps local accumulated coverage. Flow accumulates per dab, whole-stroke opacity caps the result. Color is normalized straight encoded document working RGBA. Pixel color blends in linear P3, preserving untouched source samples exactly; transparency lock preserves original alpha and excludes transparent pixels. Mask target blends toward normalized value, respects linked mask anchors and does not alter source pixels. Clip to artboard, current u16 selection, and existing mask bounds. Pixel/all and ancestor locks apply. At most one million dabs, 250 million bounded dab-pixel visits and 250 million tile-dab tests, shared transient/source budget and cancellation. Atomic, one history entry, not batchable. 中文：画笔、绘画、蒙版绘制、压感、流量、硬度、间距。

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "BrushStroke",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "clone_source": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/CloneSource"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ],
      "default": null
    },
    "color": {
      "type": "array",
      "default": [
        0.0,
        0.0,
        0.0,
        1.0
      ],
      "items": {
        "type": "number",
        "format": "double"
      },
      "maxItems": 4,
      "minItems": 4
    },
    "flow": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 1.0
    },
    "hardness": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 0.8
    },
    "layer": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 1.0
    },
    "points": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/$defs/BrushPoint"
      }
    },
    "pressure_opacity": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "pressure_size": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "size": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 24.0
    },
    "spacing": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 0.2
    },
    "target": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/BrushTarget",
      "default": "pixels"
    },
    "value": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 1.0
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layer",
    "points"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "BrushPoint": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "pressure": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double",
          "default": 1.0
        },
        "x": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double"
        },
        "y": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "x",
        "y"
      ]
    },
    "BrushTarget": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "pixels",
        "mask"
      ]
    },
    "CloneSource": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "destination": {
          "type": [
            "array",
            "null"
          ],
          "default": null,
          "items": {
            "type": "number",
            "format": "double"
          },
          "maxItems": 2,
          "minItems": 2
        },
        "sample": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/SelectionSample",
          "default": "current_layer"
        },
        "source": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "number",
            "format": "double"
          },
          "maxItems": 2,
          "minItems": 2
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "source"
      ]
    },
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    },
    "SelectionSample": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "merged",
        "current_layer"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

## `paint.erase` — 橡皮擦笔触

Erase one arc-length round brush stroke with the same strict geometry/pressure/coverage parameters as paint.stroke. Pixel target changes alpha only and keeps hidden RGB codes exactly; transparency lock rejects erasing. Mask target paints toward normalized value (default white=1); the native eraser uses background-color luminance. Color is ignored. Current selection, linked mask bounds, pixel/ancestor locks, cancellation, shared memory and work limits apply. Atomic one history entry; not batchable. 中文：橡皮擦、擦除、蒙版绘制。

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

```json
    {
```

```json
      "x": 8.5,
      "y": 8.5
    }
  ],
  "pressure_size": false,
  "size": 4,
  "target": "mask",
  "value": 1.0
}
```

## `paint.sample_color` — 吸取颜色

Read encoded working RGBA at an artboard canvas point; sample merged visible composite or visible active raw pixel layer. Radius 0..31; larger neighborhoods are alpha-weighted in linear P3, then converted through the actual working ICC; output alpha is mean alpha. Raw current-layer radius zero preserves exact source-depth values including hidden RGB. Query does not edit document/history/clipboard. 中文：吸管、取色、颜色取样。

Kind: `Query` · Undoable: `false` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.read`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "SampleColor",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "radius": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint32",
      "default": 0,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "sample": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/SelectionSample",
      "default": "merged"
    },
    "x": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    },
    "y": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "x",
    "y"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "SelectionSample": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "merged",
        "current_layer"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json

  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "SampleColorResult",
  "type": "object",

      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint8",
      "maximum": 255,
      "minimum": 0

        "type": "number",
        "format": "double"

      "minItems": 4

      "type": "string"
    },

      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint",
      "minimum": 0
    }

  "required": [
    "color",
    "bit_depth",
    "profile",

}
```

```json
  "sample": "merged",
  "x": 4,
  "y": 4
}
```

## `paint.fill` — 填充像素

Fill an explicit unlocked pixel layer inside the artboard and current u16 selection, normalized working RGBA and opacity 0..1. Straight colors convert through actual working ICC and composite in linear P3; source samples at zero selection coverage remain exact. Transparency lock preserves alpha/excludes transparent pixels; ancestor locks, cancellation, shared tile budgets and one atomic history apply. Not batchable. 中文：填充、前景色、背景色。

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

```json

      },
      "maxItems": 4,

    },
    "layer": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 1.0
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layer",
    "color"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### Result

```json

  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",

  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "layer_id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid"
```

## `paint.bucket` — 油漆桶

Compute wand-style tolerance 0..255 and four-connected/global matches from merged or current-layer working samples, then fill the explicit pixel target through that region and current selection. ## `paint.gradient` — 渐变像素

Paint linear/radial/angle/reflected/diamond gradient on an explicit pixel layer, artboard and current selection. Start/end are bounded canvas positions and must differ. At most 64 strictly increasing stops spanning at=0..1; normalized working RGBA. Colors interpolate in premultiplied encoded working RGB to avoid transparent hidden-color halos, then composite in linear P3 with opacity 0..1; reverse optional. Shape coordinates use pixel centers: linear projection, radial distance, signed angle modulo one turn, reflected absolute projection, diamond L1 distance. Fill locks/cancellation/shared budgets/atomic history apply. 中文：线性、径向、角度、对称、菱形渐变。

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

## `retouch.clone` — 仿制图章笔触

Clone from an immutable input snapshot during one pixel brush stroke. Required clone_source.source is canvas [x,y]; destination is the canvas anchor for fixed alignment, default first stroke point. Sampling maps target + source - destination, bilinear premultiplied linear P3 with out-of-artboard transparent; sample=current_layer uses raw target pixel layer, sample=merged uses the visible composite. Source colors never feed back from just-written target samples. Same size/hardness/opacity/flow/spacing/pressure/selection/transparency and ancestor lock semantics as paint.stroke. Pixel target only; color/value ignored, clone_source not allowed on ordinary brushes. At most 8192 points, bounded dab work and tile cache; shared budgets and cancellation apply. Atomic one history edit. 中文：仿制图章、取样点、对齐、所有图层取样。

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "BrushStroke",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "clone_source": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/CloneSource"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ],
      "default": null
    },
    "color": {
      "type": "array",
      "default": [
        0.0,
        0.0,
        0.0,
        1.0
      ],
      "items": {
        "type": "number",
        "format": "double"
      },
      "maxItems": 4,
      "minItems": 4
    },
    "flow": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 1.0
    },
    "hardness": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 0.8
    },
    "layer": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/LayerRef"
    },
    "opacity": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 1.0
    },
    "points": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/$defs/BrushPoint"
      }
    },
    "pressure_opacity": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "pressure_size": {
      "type": "boolean",
      "default": true
    },
    "size": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 24.0
    },
    "spacing": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 0.2
    },
    "target": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/BrushTarget",
      "default": "pixels"
    },
    "value": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 1.0
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "layer",
    "points"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "BrushPoint": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "pressure": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double",
          "default": 1.0
        },
        "x": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double"
        },
        "y": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "x",
        "y"
      ]
    },
    "BrushTarget": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "pixels",
        "mask"
      ]
    },
    "CloneSource": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "destination": {
          "type": [
            "array",
            "null"
          ],
          "default": null,
          "items": {
            "type": "number",
            "format": "double"
          },
          "maxItems": 2,
          "minItems": 2
        },
        "sample": {
          "$ref": "#/$defs/SelectionSample",
          "default": "current_layer"
        },
        "source": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "number",
            "format": "double"
          },
          "maxItems": 2,
          "minItems": 2
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "source"
      ]
    },
    "LayerRef": {
      "description": "Stable ID preferred. Duplicate names return candidates instead of guessing.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "id"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "name": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "name"
          ]
        }
      ]
    },
    "SelectionSample": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "merged",
        "current_layer"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

## `retouch.heal` — 修复画笔笔触

Transfer source texture while solving linear-P3 Poisson gradients against an immutable target Dirichlet boundary. Required stroke uses the same round brush/selection/pressure/opacity/flow/locks as paint.stroke, pixel target and explicit clone_source as retouch.clone; color/value ignored. source/current_layer requires nontransparent target boundary; merged sampling can use the visible composite for source and destination reference. Transparent source or destination samples are excluded from the solved domain. Footprint plus 1px boundary at most 262144 pixels. Conjugate gradient max_iterations 1..2048 (default 512), RMS tolerance 1e-7..0.001 (default 0.00005); nonconvergence returns error without editing. Soft coverage and selection applied once, source samples unchanged outside it. Finite solved colors clamp to working RGB gamut at publication; source alpha blends over the target. Shared memory and cancellation throughout sampling/solve/publish; one atomic history item, not batchable. This is sampled gradient-domain healing; automatic spot matching is a separate command. 中文：修复画笔、源纹理、边界融合。

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.

### Parameters

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "HealStroke",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "max_iterations": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "uint32",
      "default": 512,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "stroke": {
      "$ref": "#/$defs/BrushStroke"
    },
    "tolerance": {
      "type": "number",
      "format": "double",
      "default": 0.00005
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "stroke"
  ],
  "$defs": {
    "BrushPoint": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "pressure": {
          "type": "number",
          "format": "double",
          "default": 1.0
        },
        "x": {
```

## `retouch.spot_heal` — 污点修复笔触

Automatically match nearby intact texture outside a round brush footprint, then apply retouch.heal Poisson boundary fusion. Nested stroke has a pixel target and no clone_source; brush/selection/locks/pressure/flow/opacity and atomic one-history semantics remain. Sample current_layer (default raw target) or merged visible composite. Search radius 1..512px (default 64), deterministic coarse translation grid with local refinement, at most 2048 offsets and 128 nontransparent boundary/32 interior samples; search decodes at most 1024 source tiles and caches eight. Source and destination footprint bounding rectangles plus boundary never overlap and source stays inside artboard. Score is boundary linear-P3 RGB variance after mean offset plus 5% mean-color error; nearest offset breaks numerical ties. Return chosen canvas source anchor, candidate count, RMS boundary error and healing diagnostics. Footprint plus 1px boundary <=262144 pixels; max_iterations/tolerance follow retouch.heal. Missing usable source, nonconvergence, cancellation or shared memory failure do not edit the document. This is proximity texture matching; content-aware PatchMatch synthesis is tracked separately. 中文：污点修复、自动近邻纹理匹配、边界融合。

Kind: `Mutate` · Undoable: `true` · Batchable: `false`

Effects: `document.write`

Permissions: `bound_document`

- Host-bound source, session, document and revision; control ownership and resource limits are checked.
